,RGYUD KYI RGYAL PO CHEN PO DPAL YANG DAG PAR SBYOR BA'I THIG LE ZHES BYA BA RDZOGS SO,,
,,RGYA GAR GYI MKHAN PO K'A YA STHA PA GA YA DHA RA'I ZHAL SNGA NAS DANG, BOD KYI LOTZTSA BA DGE SLONG SH'AKYA YE SHES KYIS BSGYUR CING ZHUS TE GTAN LA PHAB PA, SLAD NAS KYANG BOD KYI LOTZTSA BA GZHAN RNAMS KYIS BSGYUR BA LA ZUR MI 'DRA BAR BCOS NAS RANG GI MING BCUG CING, GZHAN GYI MING 'PHYI PA DE DAG DANG, MI SHES PA DE DAG SUN DBYUNG BA'I PHYIR SLAD NAS BYANG RGYA GAR GYI RGYUD BZHI DANG, ,DGE SLONG SH'AKYA YE SHES DE NYID KYIS GTUGS NAS BCOS TE GTAN LA PHAB PA'O,,
